Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The core variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 564 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 428 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_564(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 564. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^428(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The core variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 973 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 41 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 973 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the core structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 41. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
